Theorem 234.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the Convergence Analysis assumption on complex structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 705. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 616 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: Consider the Convergence Analysis operator L on the complex space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 705 for all k. By the Convergence Analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 616. QED.

Theorem 234.2 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a complex algebra A over field F with Convergence Analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 616. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 108/2.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the Convergence Analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_616(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 616. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^108(S, F) is trivial. QED.
